# Page map: the original, the static archive, the Drupal 11 rebuild

Generated by `migration/page-map.mjs` from the frozen baseline. Every page links to the same path on
the original (https://hobobiker-copy--remotework--rfay.coder.ddev.com), the static archive (https://static.hobobiker.com) and the rebuild (https://hobobiker-d11--remotework--rfay.coder.ddev.com).
Unpublished nodes answer 403 on the original and the rebuild, 404 in the archive (declared for the static target).
The archive is GitHub Pages, which ignores `?page=N` (it serves page 1).
